The method, which requires no additional structural assumptions on the error distribution, yields uniform improvements on standard local polynomial estimators, and comes within small constant factors of minimax lower bounds. We believe the work opens the door to an exploration of the phenomenon of distributional adaptivity in related statistical problems.

Funding.

EHY and RJS were supported by European Research Council Advanced Grant 101019498.

Appendix A
Proof of Theorem
1

We introduce the following additional notation for the proof of Theorem
1
.
For
\alpha=(\alpha_{1},\ldots,\alpha_{d})^{\top}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}
, define the
multi-index factorial

\alpha!:=\prod_{r=1}^{d}\alpha_{r}!
.
Given a symmetric matrix
M\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times m}
, we write its minimum eigenvalue as
\Lambda_{\min}(M)
. Recall that our cross-fitted estimator (Algorithm
1
) is over
\mathcal{K}\geq 2
folds, for which we define the number of observations per fold as
n_{k}:=|\mathcal{I}_{k}|=\lfloor n/\mathcal{K}\rfloor
for
k\in[\mathcal{K}]
. It will be convenient to introduce the shorthands
\iota_{h}(\cdot):=K_{h}(\cdot)Q_{h}(\cdot)
and
\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\cdot):=K_{h}^{t}(\cdot)Q_{h}(\cdot)Q_{h}(\cdot)^{\top}
, for
t\in\{1,2\}
and
p_{0}:=p_{X}(x_{0})
. For
m\in[\bar{p}]
, we write
\mathrm{e}_{m}\in\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}
for the
m
th standard basis vector, and define the shorthands
\beta_{0}^{*}:=\lceil\beta^{*}\rceil-1
and

\displaystyle\zeta_{\rho^{2}}(x):=\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\rho^{2}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,|\,X=x\},\qquad\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x):=\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,|\,X=x\},

(27)

\displaystyle\zeta_{(\rho^{\prime})^{2}}(x):=\mathbb{E}_{P}\{(\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X))^{2}\,|\,X=x\},

(28)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x):=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,\big|\,X=x,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\},\quad\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k}(x):=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,\big|\,X=x,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\},

(29)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k,\text{sq}}(x):=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)^{2}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,\big|\,X=x,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\},\quad\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\text{sq}}(x):=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})^{2}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,\big|\,X=x,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\},

(30)

for
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
k\in[\mathcal{K}]
.
For a Borel measurable function
\kappa:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
and
t\geq 1
, it will help to define

R_{t}(\kappa):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|\kappa(\nu)|^{t}\,d\nu.

Proof of Theorem
1
.

Fix
x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}
. Define coefficients
\theta_{0}=\bigl(f(x_{0}),0,\ldots,0\bigr)^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}
, the local error term
e_{0}(x):=f(x)-f(x_{0})
, the population level orthogonaliser

\mu(x_{0}):=\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\big)}{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\big)},

and, for
k\in[\mathcal{K}]
,

c_{k}(x_{0}):=\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\big(f(X)-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)\big)\,\big|\,\hat{f}_{k}^{\mathrm{LP}}\big\}}{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\big)}.

Let
a_{n}:=\inf\mathcal{H}_{n}
and
b_{n}:=\sup\mathcal{H}_{n}
. Note that by Assumption
(A2.3)
these sequences satisfy
0<a_{n}\leq b_{n}
,
b_{n}\to 0
and
na_{n}^{d}\to\infty
.

For
k\in[\mathcal{K}]
, define functions on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
given by

\hat{\varphi}_{k}(\cdot):=\iota_{h}(\cdot-x_{0})-\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(\cdot-x_{0}),

where
\hat{\mu}_{k}
is defined in Algorithm
1
, and the analogous population level version

\varphi(\cdot):=\iota_{h}(\cdot-x_{0})-\mu(x_{0})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(\cdot-x_{0}).

Finally, let

\displaystyle\varrho_{i}:=\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(X_{i})Q_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\top}(\hat{\theta}-\theta_{0})

\displaystyle-\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(X_{i})e_{0}(X_{i})

\displaystyle+\bigl(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\bigr)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(\lambda h)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(X_{i})

for
k\in[\mathcal{K}]
and
i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}
. Then, by a Taylor expansion with the mean value form of the remainder, there exists
\boldsymbol{\tau}=(\tau_{i})_{i\in[n]}\in[0,1]^{n}
such that
\hat{\theta}
in Algorithm
1
satisfies

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}\Big(Y_{i}-\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(X_{i})Q_{h}(X_{i}\!-\!x_{0})^{\top}\hat{\theta}-\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\big)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(\lambda h)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(X_{i})\,\Big|\,X_{i}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}(\varepsilon_{i}-\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\varrho_{i}.

(31)

Rearranging, we obtain

\displaystyle\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})(\hat{\theta}-\theta_{0})=\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}}(\boldsymbol{\tau}),

(32)

where

\displaystyle\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i}),

(33)

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i}),

(34)

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})e_{0}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i}),

(35)

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigl\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigr\}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\big)\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})

(36)

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\big)\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0}),

(37)

and where in the above rearrangement we used the identities

\displaystyle\hat{\varphi}_{k}(\cdot)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(\cdot)

\displaystyle=\iota_{h}(\cdot-x_{0}),

\displaystyle\hat{\varphi}_{k}(\cdot)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(\lambda h)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(\cdot)

\displaystyle=-\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(\cdot-x_{0}),

\displaystyle\iota_{h}(\cdot)Q_{h}(\cdot)^{\top}

\displaystyle={\pi_{h}}(\cdot).

By Lemma
14
,
\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})
is invertible with probability
1-o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
. Also by Lemma
14
,

\displaystyle\hat{f}(x_{0})-f(x_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}-\theta_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\big(\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})\big)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})+(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1))\,\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\big(\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})+\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})\big),

(38)

where
\Omega:=p_{0}s_{1}(K)\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})
.
Then each error term may be dealt with in turn:

(i)

By Lemma
8
,

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}=\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\phi_{\textup{I}}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg),\qquad\phi_{\textup{I}}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varphi(X_{i})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i}).

(ii)

By Lemma
11
,

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=B(f,x_{0},K,h)h^{\beta^{*}}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big),

where

B(f,x_{0},K,h):=h^{-\beta^{*}}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)\bigl\{f(x_{0}+h\nu)-f(x_{0})\bigr\}\,d\nu

(39)

for
\beta_{0}^{*}=0
and

\displaystyle B(f,x_{0},K,h):=h^{\beta_{0}^{*}-\beta^{*}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{\beta_{0}^{*}}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)\nu^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\cdot\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\beta_{0}^{*}-1}\bigl\{\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0}+th\nu)-\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0})\bigr\}\,dt\,d\nu

(40)

otherwise.

(iii)

By Lemma
12
,

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg).

(iv)

By Lemma
13
,

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\varepsilon_{i}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg).

Combining all of the above with (
38
),

\displaystyle\hat{f}(x_{0})-f(x_{0})=B(f,x_{0},K,h)h^{\beta^{*}}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{n,i}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg),

(41)

\displaystyle Z_{n,i}:=\frac{1}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\varphi(X_{i})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})+\frac{1}{p_{0}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\varepsilon_{i}.

(42)

By Lemma
18
,

\lim_{n\to\infty}\sup_{P\in\mathcal{P}}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}_{n}}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{P}\biggl(\bigg(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\bigg\{\frac{R_{2}(K)V_{P,x_{0},\lambda}^{\mathrm{finite}}(\rho)}{p_{X}(x_{0})}\bigg\}\bigg)^{-1/2}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{n,i}\bigg)\leq t\biggr)-\Phi(t)\bigg|=0,

(43)

where

V_{P,x_{0},\lambda}^{\mathrm{finite}}(\rho):=V_{P}(\rho)+\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{R_{2}(K)}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\biggl(\frac{\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)}{\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}-\varepsilon\bigg)^{2}\,\bigg|\,X=x_{0}\biggr\}.

Finally, as
\lambda\to\infty
,
R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\to 0
, thus

V_{P,x_{0},\lambda}^{\mathrm{finite}}(\rho)=V_{P}(\rho)+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
.
∎

Lemma 8
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
, and with
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}
as defined in (
34
),

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}=\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\phi_{\textup{I}}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg),

where
\phi_{\textup{I}}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varphi(X_{i})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})
.

Proof.

We decompose

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varphi(X_{i})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big(\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})-\varphi(X_{i})\big)\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\varphi(X_{i})(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big(\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})-\varphi(X_{i})\big)(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varphi(X_{i})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})}_{=:\phi_{\textup{I}}}-\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\big(\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})-\mu(x_{0})\big)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{i}.k}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\varphi(X_{i})(\hat{\rho}_{k}\!-\!\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{ii}.k}}\!-\!\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\big(\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\!-\!\mu(x_{0})\big)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}\!-\!x_{0})(\hat{\rho}_{k}\!-\!\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{iii}.k}}.

(44)

We show each of the terms
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{i}.k},\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{ii}.k},\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{iii}.k}
is
o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
in turn. Define
\eta:=\Omega^{-1}\mathrm{e}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}
.

Term
\boldsymbol{\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{i}.k}}
:

For
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+b_{n}\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}

\displaystyle\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X_{i}-x_{0})\rho^{2}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg\}+\biggl(\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg\}}_{=0\text{ as }\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,|\,X\}=0}\biggr)^{2}

(45)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\rho^{2}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}\kappa_{\lambda}^{2}(\nu)\zeta_{\rho^{2}}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}^{2/(2+\delta)}C_{X}}{nh^{d}}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{nh^{d}}\bigg).

Thus
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)
, and so together with Lemma
9
,

\eta^{\top}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{i}.k}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg)O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg)=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{nh^{d}}\bigg)=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg).

Term
\boldsymbol{\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{ii}.k}}
:

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{

\displaystyle(\eta^{\top}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{ii}.k})^{2}\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\bigl(\eta^{\top}\varphi(X_{i})\bigr)^{2}(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)^{2}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}\setminus\{i\}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\eta^{\top}\varphi(X_{i})(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\eta^{\top}\varphi(X_{j})(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon_{j}\,|\,X_{j})\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\bigl(\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)\bigr)^{2}(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)^{2}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}+\Big(\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\bigl(\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)\bigr)^{2}\,\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k,\text{sq}}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}+\Big(\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)\,\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}\Big)^{2}.

(46)

For the first term in (
46
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\bigl(\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)\bigr)^{2}\bigr\}

\displaystyle\leq\|\eta\|_{2}^{2}\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\|\varphi(X)\|_{2}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\|\eta\|_{2}^{2}\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\Bigl(K_{h}^{2}(X-x_{0})\|Q_{h}(X-x_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mu(x_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\,\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{c_{X}^{2}c_{1}^{2}\Lambda_{\min}^{2}\bigl(s_{1}(K)\bigr)}\Bigl(e^{d}\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(K_{h}^{2}(X-x_{0})\bigr)+\|\mu(x_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\bigr)\Bigr)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigl(h^{-d}\bigr),

where we applied Lemma
16
in the penultimate line.
Thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\bigl(\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)\bigr)^{2}\,\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k,\text{sq}}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{(\eta^{\top}\varphi(X))^{2}\bigr\}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}+\mathcal{B}_{0}(b_{n})}\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k,\mathrm{sq}}(x)=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{-d}\big).

(47)

For the second term in (
46
), define
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x):=\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x)-\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0})
.
We remark that in the special case where
L_{\mathcal{E}}=0
(which holds if
\varepsilon\mathrel{\perp\!\!\!\perp}X
), we simply obtain
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x)=0
and so
\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(\cdot)
is constant, which together with
\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\varphi(X)\}=0
means that the second term in (
46
) is zero.
In general, however, we accommodate
\varepsilon\not\hskip-1.00006pt\mathrel{\perp\!\!\!\perp}X
(i.e.
L_{\mathcal{E}}>0
).

Observing that
\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\varphi(X)\}=0
by definition of
\mu(\cdot)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}

\displaystyle=\eta^{\top}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\bigr\}-\eta^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt-\eta^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}\kappa_{\lambda}(\nu)\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)d\nu

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt-\eta^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}\kappa_{\lambda}(\nu)\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu,

where the third equality makes use of the fact that
s_{1}(K)\mathrm{e}_{1}=\mathrm{e}_{1}
for our order
p+1
kernel, so that

\eta^{\top}Q(\nu)=\frac{\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}s_{1}(K)^{-1}Q(\nu)}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}=\frac{\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}Q(\nu)}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}=\frac{1}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}.

Define
\bar{\beta}:=\beta_{X}\wedge\beta_{\mathcal{E}}
and
\bar{\beta}_{0}:=\lceil\bar{\beta}\rceil-1
. Then
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}p_{X}\in\mathcal{H}(\bar{\beta},\bar{L})
on
\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
for some
\bar{L}\in(0,\infty)
that depends only on
L_{X},L_{\mathcal{E}},\beta_{X},\beta_{\mathcal{E}}
.
Moreover,
\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0})p_{X}(x_{0})=0
. By a Taylor expansion, for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, there exists
t_{x}\in[0,1]
such that

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x)p_{X}(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
1\leq\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq\bar{\beta}_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\big(\partial^{\alpha}(\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}p_{X})(x_{0})\big)(x-x_{0})^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\bar{\beta}_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\Big(\partial^{\alpha}\bigl(\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}p_{X})\bigl(x_{0}+t_{x}(x-x_{0})\bigr)-\partial^{\alpha}(\bar{\mathcal{E}}p_{X})(x_{0})\Bigr)(x-x_{0})^{\alpha}.

Then since
K
is a kernel of order
p+1\geq\lceil\bar{\beta}\rceil
, and making use of (
65
),

\displaystyle\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}

\displaystyle K(\nu)\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu\bigg|

\displaystyle=h^{\bar{\beta}_{0}}\biggl|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\bar{\beta}_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)\big\{\partial^{\alpha}(\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}p_{X})(x_{0}+t_{x_{0}+h\nu}h\nu)-\partial^{\alpha}(\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}p_{X})(x_{0})\big\}\nu^{\alpha}\,d\nu\biggr|

\displaystyle\leq\bar{L}\,h^{\bar{\beta}_{0}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\bar{\beta}_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}|K(\nu)\nu^{\alpha}|\cdot\|h\nu\|^{\bar{\beta}-\bar{\beta}_{0}}\,d\nu\leq\frac{\bar{L}R_{1}(K)d^{\bar{\beta}_{0}}}{\bar{\beta}_{0}!}h^{\bar{\beta}}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\bar{\beta}}\big).

Furthermore, by similar arguments, and since

\kappa_{\lambda}
is a kernel of order
\lceil\bar{\beta}\rceil
,
it follows that

\displaystyle\bigg|\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}\kappa_{\lambda}(\nu)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{E}}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bar{L}\,h^{\bar{\beta}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\bar{\beta}_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}|\kappa_{\lambda}(\nu)\nu^{\alpha}|\cdot\|\nu\|^{\bar{\beta}-\bar{\beta}_{0}}\,d\nu\leq\frac{\bar{L}R_{1}(\kappa)d^{\bar{\beta}_{0}}}{\bar{\beta}_{0}!}(\lambda h)^{\bar{\beta}}.

Therefore

\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\eta^{\top}\varphi(X)\,\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big((\lambda h)^{\bar{\beta}}\big).

Combining this with (
46
) and (
47
),

\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{(\eta^{\top}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{ii}.k})^{2}\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\big\}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\bigg)+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big((\lambda h)^{2\bar{\beta}}\big)=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}+h^{2\beta^{*}}\bigg).

Term
\boldsymbol{\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{iii}.k}}
:
First,

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}

\displaystyle\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\,\bigg|\,\hat{\rho}_{k},(X_{i})_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X_{i})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigl|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\bigr|\cdot|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0})|+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\cdot\big|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(X_{i})-\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0})\big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigl|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\bigr|\cdot|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0})|+L_{\mathcal{E}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\cdot\|X_{i}-x_{0}\|^{\beta_{\mathcal{E}}\wedge 1}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\bigl\{|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k}(x_{0})|+L_{\mathcal{E}}(\lambda h)^{\beta_{\mathcal{E}}\wedge 1}\bigr\}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigl|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\bigr|.

Now for
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+\lambda b_{n}\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigl|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\bigr|\bigg\}\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})|\bigr\}=\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}|\kappa_{\lambda}(\nu)|p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu\leq C_{X}R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda}),

and so
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)
.
Thus, together with Assumption
A1
,

\bigg|\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\,\bigg|\,\hat{\rho}_{k},(X_{i})_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigg\}\bigg|=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigl(R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigr),

and so

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})(\hat{\rho}_{k}-\rho)(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigl(R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigr).

Finally, then, we conclude by Lemma
9
that

\hat{\phi}_{\textup{I}.\mathrm{iii}.k}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg)o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg)=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg),

as required.
∎

Lemma 9
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
, for each
k\in[\mathcal{K}]
,

\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})-\mu(x_{0})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg).

Proof.

We consider the numerator and denominator term in
\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})
separately. For the numerator, by Lemma
16
,

\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\bigg\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})-\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}\bigg\|_{2}^{2}\bigg\}\leq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\|\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\big\}\\
=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|h^{d}}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K^{2}(\nu)\|Q(\nu)\|_{2}^{2}\,p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu\leq\frac{C_{X}e^{d}R_{2}(K)}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|h^{d}}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\bigg),

and so

\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})=\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg).

For the denominator,

\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})-\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}\leq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\}\\
\leq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|h^{d}}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}\kappa_{\lambda}^{2}(\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu\leq\frac{C_{X}}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|h^{d}}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{nh^{d}}\bigg),

so

\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})=\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg).

(48)

Therefore, using the fact that
\sup_{\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}(K)}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})=R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda_{0}(K)})=R_{2}(K)<\infty
, we have

\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})=\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\}\big\{1+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big((nh^{d})^{-1/2}\big)\big\}}{\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\}\big\{1+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big((nh^{d})^{-1/2}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)\big\}}=\mu(x_{0})\big\{1+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big((nh^{d})^{-1/2}\big)\big\}.

Finally, as

\mu(x_{0})=\frac{\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)Q(\nu)\,d\nu}{\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}\kappa_{\lambda}(\nu)\,d\nu}\big\{1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\big\}=u(K)\big\{1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\big\},

(49)

we have

\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})-\mu(x_{0})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg),

as required.
∎

Lemma 10
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
, for each
k\in[\mathcal{K}]
,

\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg).

Proof.

We consider the numerator and denominator in
\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})
separately.
For the numerator,

\displaystyle\quad\;\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big\{Y_{i}-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})\big\}-\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\big\{Y-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)\big\}\,\Big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\Big)\bigg)^{2}\,\bigg|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\{Y-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)\big\}^{2}\,\Big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\mathbb{E}_{P}(\varepsilon^{2}\,|\,X)\big)+\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\{\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)\big\}^{2}\,\big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\big)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\bigg\{\bigl(C_{3}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}+\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\{\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)\big\}^{2}\,\big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\big)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{nh^{d}}\bigg),

where the final line follows using the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Bigl\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\bigl\{\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)\bigr\}^{2}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\bigl(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(x)-f(x)\bigr)^{2}\Big\},

and Lemma
21
.
Therefore

\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big\{Y_{i}-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})\big\}=\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\big\{Y-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)\big\}\,\Big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\Big)+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg).

The denominator is the same as the denominator in
\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})
; see (
48
).
Thus,

\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})=c_{k}(x_{0})\bigg\{1+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg)\bigg\}.

Now,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(|c_{k}(x_{0})|\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})|\cdot\bigl|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)\bigr|\big\}}{|\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\}|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P}\{|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})|\}}{|\mathbb{E}_{P}\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\}|}\cdot\sup_{x\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(\lambda h)}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\bigl|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(x)-f(x)\bigr|\bigr\}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg),

(50)

where we use Jensen’s inequality and Lemma
21
.
Thus

\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg),

as required.
∎

Lemma 11
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
, and with
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})
defined in (
35
) and
B(f,x_{0},K,h)
defined as in (
40
),

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)B(f,x_{0},K,h)h^{\beta^{*}}.

Proof.

For all
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}
with
\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq\beta_{0}^{*}
, and writing
m_{\alpha}\in[\bar{p}]
as the index such that
\{Q(u)\}_{m_{\alpha}}=\frac{1}{\alpha!}u^{\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\quad\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\alpha}\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg)

\displaystyle=\alpha!h^{\|\alpha\|_{1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg)\mathrm{e}_{m_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\alpha!h^{\|\alpha\|_{1}}\mathrm{e}_{m_{\alpha}},

where we use the fact that
u^{\alpha}=\alpha!h^{\|\alpha\|_{1}}Q_{h}(u)^{\top}\mathrm{e}_{m_{\alpha}}
. As the components of
Q
are in lexicographical order, for any
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}
with
1\leq\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq\beta_{0}^{*}
,

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\alpha}\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigg)=0.

(51)

By taking a Taylor expansion with an integral form of remainder, and noticing that
e_{0}(x_{0})=0
, we obtain the decomposition

\displaystyle e_{0}(X_{i})

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
1\leq\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\,\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0})(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\alpha}+e^{*}_{0}(X_{i})

where

\displaystyle e_{0}^{*}(X_{i})

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{\beta_{0}^{*}}{\alpha!}\bigg(\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\beta_{0}^{*}-1}\bigl\{\partial^{\alpha}f\bigl(x_{0}+t(X_{i}-x_{0})\bigr)-\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0})\bigr\}\,dt\bigg)(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\alpha}.

(52)

In combination with (
51
), this yields

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}^{*}(\boldsymbol{\tau}),

(53)

where

\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}^{*}(\boldsymbol{\tau}):=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})e_{0}^{*}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i}).

Now, by Lemma
14
,

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}^{*}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}^{*}(\boldsymbol{\tau}),

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}^{*}(\boldsymbol{\tau})

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big)e_{0}^{*}(X_{i})\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})}_{=:\phi_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{i}}^{*}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big)e_{0}^{*}(X_{i})\big\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\big\}}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{ii}.k}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big)e_{0}^{*}(X_{i})\big\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\big\}}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{iii}.k}}.

(54)

We proceed by treating each term in the above decomposition separately.

Term
\boldsymbol{\phi_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{i}}^{*}}
:

Recalling the definition of
C_{e^{*}}
from Lemma
17
, we have

\displaystyle\Var_{P}(\phi_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{i}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\Var_{P}\Big(\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\big)e_{0}^{*}(X)\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\big)^{2}\bigl\{e_{0}^{*}(X)\bigr\}^{2}\bigl\{\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\bigr\}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}C_{e^{*}}^{2}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{2\beta^{*}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\big)^{2}\zeta_{(\rho^{\prime})^{2}}(X)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{e^{*}}^{2}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{2\beta^{*}}}{p_{0}^{2}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}^{2}(x_{0})nh^{d}}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K^{2}(\nu)\zeta_{(\rho^{\prime})^{2}}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{X}C_{2}R_{2}(K)C_{e^{*}}^{2}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{2\beta^{*}}}{c_{1}^{2}p_{0}^{2}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}^{2}(x_{0})nh^{d}}\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{h^{2\beta^{*}}}{nh^{d}}\bigg),

by Lemmas
17
and
16
.
Therefore, by Chebychev’s inequality,

\displaystyle\phi_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{i}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\big)e_{0}^{*}(X)\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\big\}+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{h^{\beta^{*}}}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}s_{1}(K)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})e_{0}^{*}(X)\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\big\}+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{h^{\beta^{*}}}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{K_{h}(X-x_{0})e_{0}^{*}(X)\bigr\}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big),

where the final line follows by uniform continuity of
\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}
.

Term
\boldsymbol{\phi_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{ii}.k}^{*}}
:

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k,\mathrm{abs}}
from (
30
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(|\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{ii}.k}

\displaystyle|\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\bigr)\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\big|\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\big|\cdot|e_{0}^{*}(X)|\cdot\bigl|(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\bigr|\,\Big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{e^{*}}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{\beta^{*}}}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\mathbb{E}_{P}\{|K_{h}(X-x_{0})|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho},k,\text{sq}}^{1/2}(X)\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{X}R_{1}(K)C_{e^{*}}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{\beta^{*}}}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1))\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})^{2}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k},X=x\big\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big),

by Lemma
17
and Assumption
A1
, so
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{ii}.k}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big)
.

Term
\boldsymbol{\phi_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{iii}.k}^{*}}
:

As
\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}
is uniformly continuous by Assumption
A1
, the function
\omega(\Delta):=\sup\{|\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(x)-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|:x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\,\|x-x^{\prime}\|\leq\Delta\}
satisfies
\lim_{\Delta\searrow 0}\omega(\Delta)=0
. Hence

\displaystyle|\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{iii}.k}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big(\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big)e_{0}^{*}(X_{i})\big\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\big\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big|\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big|\cdot|e_{0}^{*}(X_{i})|\cdot\bigl|\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{e^{*}}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{\beta^{*}}}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\cdot\omega\bigl(|\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}|\bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{e^{*}}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{\beta^{*}}}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\omega\Bigl(\max_{i:X_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}|\varrho_{i}|\Bigr)\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|.

(55)

Now,
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
, and

\displaystyle\max_{i:X_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}|\varrho_{i}|

\displaystyle=\max_{i:X_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}\bigl|Q_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\top}(\hat{\theta}-\theta_{0})-e_{0}(X_{i})\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq e^{d/2}\|\hat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\|_{2}+C_{e}(L,d,\beta^{*},h)h^{1\vee\beta^{*}}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1),

(56)

by Cauchy–Schwarz and Lemmas
17
,
16
, and
20
. It follows that
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}.\mathrm{iii}.k}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big)
.

We conclude from (
53
) and (
54
) that

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{II}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=\frac{1}{p_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{K_{h}(X-x_{0})e_{0}^{*}(X)\bigr\}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big).

Finally, applying Lemma
15
gives the result.
∎

Lemma 12
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
, and with
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}
as defined in (
36
),

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg).

Proof.

We decompose

\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\bigl\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i})\bigr\}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\big)\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}.k}}.

Term
\boldsymbol{\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}.k}}
:

For
i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}
,

\varrho_{i}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i})=\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\big)\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}).

For the modulus of continuity
\omega
of
\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}
as in (
55
) it follows that

\displaystyle\|\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}.k}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\omega\Bigl(\max_{i:X_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(\lambda h)}|\varrho_{i}|\Bigr)\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\big|\cdot|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i})|\bigg)\|\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\omega\Bigl(\max_{i:X_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(\lambda h)}|\varrho_{i}|\Bigr)\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})\big|\cdot|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i})|\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt+\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i})|\bigg)|\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})|\bigg\}\|\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\|_{2}.

By Lemma
9
, (
49
) and Lemma
16
, and then Lemma
10
and (
50
),

\|\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\|_{2}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(R_{1}(K)\big),\qquad|\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})|=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg\}R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg).

Further,
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i})|=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)
and for
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda b_{n})\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})\big|\cdot|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i})|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\big|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)\big|\cdot|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X)|\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\{|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X)|\}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}+\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda b_{n})}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\big|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(x)-f(x)\big|\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\{|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X)|\}\bigg(\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}^{\circ}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\bigl(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(x)-f(x)\bigr)^{2}\Big\}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg\}R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg),

where we applied Lemma
21
in the final line.
Thus

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big|\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})\big|\cdot|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i})|=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigg\}R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg).

By Assumption
A1
(
56
) we also have
\omega\bigl(\max_{i:X_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}|\varrho_{i}|\bigr)=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
,
so
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{III}.k}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\big)
, as required.
∎

Lemma 13
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
,

\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}}=\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\varepsilon_{i}.

Proof.

We decompose

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+c_{k}(x_{0})\big)\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{i}.k}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})\big)\big\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})-\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\mu(x_{0})\big\}}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{ii}.k}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})\big)\mu(x_{0})}_{=:\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{iii}.k}}.

Term
\boldsymbol{\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{i}.k}}
:

By construction of
c_{k}(x_{0})
, for any
i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}
,

\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+c_{k}(x_{0})\big)\,\big|\,(X_{i^{\prime}},Y_{i^{\prime}})_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\big\}=0.

Hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}

\displaystyle\bigg\{\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+c_{k}(x_{0})\big)\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\bigg\|_{2}^{2}\,\bigg|\,(X_{i^{\prime}},Y_{i^{\prime}})_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\mathbb{E}\big(\bigl\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\bigr\}^{2}\,\big|\,\hat{\rho}_{k},X\big)\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)+c_{k}(x_{0})\big)^{2}\,\Big|\,(X_{i^{\prime}},Y_{i^{\prime}})_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\Big\}\|\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\bigl(2C_{2}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)+c_{k}(x_{0})\big)^{2}\,\Big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\Big\}\|\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4C_{2}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)}{n}\biggl(\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)\big)^{2}\,\Big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt+\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}c_{k}^{2}(x_{0})\biggr)\|\mu(x_{0})\|_{2}^{2},

where the final line follows by Lemma
9
.
Further, for
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda b_{n})\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)

\displaystyle\big)^{2}\Big\}=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}+\mathcal{B}_{0}(\lambda b_{n})}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(x)-f(x)\big)^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{d}}C_{X}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}^{\circ}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(x)-f(x)\big)^{2}\Big\}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{h^{d}}\bigg),

and so
\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X))^{2}\,\big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\big\}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{-d}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)
. Moreover,
\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigl(h^{-d}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigr)
, and by (
50
) and Markov’s inequality,
c_{k}(x_{0})=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigl(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\bigr)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+c_{k}(x_{0})\big)\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})\bigg\|_{2}^{2}\,\bigg|\,(X_{i},Y_{i})_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}^{c}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\biggl\{o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{h^{d}}\bigg)+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\biggl(\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{h^{d}}\biggr)O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\biggl(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}+h^{2\beta^{*}}\biggr)\biggr\}O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)

\displaystyle=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\bigg),

and so
\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{i}.k}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\big)
.

Term
\boldsymbol{\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{ii}.k}}
:

Applying Lemmas
9
and
10
,

\displaystyle\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{ii}.k}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})}_{=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)}\bigg)\underbrace{\big(\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})\big)}_{=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)}\underbrace{\big(\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})-\mu(x_{0})\big)}_{=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\big)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg(\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\big\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\big\}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})}_{=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda}))}\bigg)\underbrace{\big(\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})\big)}_{=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\big)}\underbrace{\hat{\mu}_{k}(x_{0})}_{=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)}

\displaystyle=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{nh^{d}}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg).

Term
\boldsymbol{\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{iii}.k}}
:

We have, for
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+\lambda b_{n}\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\Var_{P}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\bigg)=\frac{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}{n^{2}}\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\zeta_{(\rho^{\prime})^{2}}(X)\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\}\leq\frac{C_{2}C_{X}}{nh^{d}}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda}),

and

\big|\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(X)\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}-p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})\big|=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1),

by dominated convergence, using the fact that
\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\bigl|\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(X)\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr|\bigr\}\leq C_{2}^{1/2}C_{X}R_{1}(\kappa_{\lambda})
.
Thus

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})=\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\frac{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}{n}p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0}).

Moreover, as
\mu(x_{0})=u(K)\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)
,

\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{iii}}:=\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{iii}.k}=\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)u(K)\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})p_{0}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\frac{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}{n}\big(\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})\big).

(57)

We further decompose

\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})={c}_{k}^{(1)}(x_{0})+c_{k}^{(2)}(x_{0})+\bigl\{{c}_{k}^{(3)}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})\bigr\},

(58)

where

\displaystyle{c}_{k}^{(1)}(x_{0})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})}\bigg(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(Y_{i}-f(X_{i})\big)\bigg),

\displaystyle{c}_{k}^{(2)}(x_{0})

\displaystyle:=\biggl\{\frac{1}{\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}\!-\!x_{0})}\!-\!\frac{1}{\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X\!-\!x_{0})\bigr)}\biggr\}\biggl(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}\!-\!x_{0})\bigl(f(X_{i})\!-\!\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})\bigr)\biggr),

\displaystyle{c}_{k}^{(3)}(x_{0})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr)}\bigg(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(f(X_{i})-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})\big)\bigg).

Now,

\displaystyle{c}_{k}^{(1)}(x_{0})

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\varepsilon_{i}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\bigg(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\varepsilon_{i}\bigg).

(59)

Next, since

\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})=\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr)+O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\biggl(\frac{R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\biggr),

and
\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr)\geq c_{X}
, we have that

\frac{1}{\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})}-\frac{1}{\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr)}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\biggl(\frac{R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\biggr).

Moreover,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\biggl(\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\biggl|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}

\displaystyle\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big(f(X_{i})-\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})\big)\biggr|\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\bigl|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigl(f(X)-\hat{f}_{k}^{\mathrm{LP}}(X)\bigr)\bigr|\bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})|\bigr)\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}^{\circ}}\Bigl(\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{f(x)-\hat{f}_{k}^{\mathrm{LP}}(x)\bigr)\bigr\}^{2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\biggl(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\biggr),

by Lemma
21
.
Thus

c_{k}^{(2)}(x_{0})=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\biggl(\biggl\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\biggr\}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\biggr).

(60)

Finally, for the third term,
\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{{c}_{k}^{(3)}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})\,\big|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}\bigr\}=0
, and

\displaystyle{c}_{k}^{(3)}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big\{\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})\,|\,\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}\bigr)\big\}}{\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})\bigr)}=:\frac{c_{N}}{c_{D}}.

Now

c_{D}=\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)p_{0},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(c_{N}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big(\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(X)-f(X)\big)^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\mathcal{I}_{k}|}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big)\bigl(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\bigr)\,\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}^{\circ}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big(\big\{\hat{f}^{\mathrm{LP}}_{k}(x)-f(x)\big\}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{nh^{d}}\bigg\{\frac{1}{nh^{d}}+h^{2\beta^{*}}\bigg\}\bigg)

by Lemma
21
. Hence

{c}_{k}^{(3)}(x_{0})-c_{k}(x_{0})=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\biggl(\biggl\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}+h^{\beta^{*}}\biggr\}R_{2}^{1/2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\biggr).

(61)

Combining (
59
), (
60
), and (
61
), we conclude that

\hat{\phi}_{\textup{IV}.\mathrm{iii}}=(1+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1))u(K)\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})p_{0}\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\varepsilon_{i},

as required. The result then follows as
\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})p_{0}\,\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}\Omega^{-1}u(K)=\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}s_{1}(K)^{-1}u(K)=1
.
∎

Lemma 14
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
,

\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau}):=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})=\Omega+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1),

where

\Omega:=p_{0}\,s_{1}(K)\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0}).

(62)

Proof.

We decompose

\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})-\Omega=\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\xi_{\textup{I}.k}+\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\xi_{\textup{II}.k}+\xi_{\textup{III}}+\xi_{\textup{IV}},

(63)

where

\displaystyle\xi_{\textup{I}.k}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\big\},

\displaystyle\xi_{\textup{II}.k}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}{\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\big\{\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\big\},

\displaystyle\xi_{\textup{III}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big({\pi_{h}}(X_{i}-x_{0})\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{{\pi_{h}}(X-x_{0})\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\big\}\Big),

\displaystyle\xi_{\textup{IV}}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{{\pi_{h}}(X-x_{0})\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\big\}-p_{0}\,s_{1}(K)\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0}).

Term
\boldsymbol{\xi_{\textup{I}.k}}
:

As in (
55
), and adopting the notation of the same modulus of continuity
\omega
,

\displaystyle\|\xi_{\textup{I}.k}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\tr^{1/2}\big(\xi_{\textup{I}.k}\xi_{\textup{I}.k}^{\top}\big)

\displaystyle\leq e^{d}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\cdot\big|\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}-\tau_{i}\varrho_{i}\,|\,X_{i})-\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})\big|\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq e^{d}\,\omega\Bigl(\max_{i:X_{i}\in\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}|\varrho_{i}|\Bigr)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1).

Term
\boldsymbol{\xi_{\textup{II}.k}}
:

Since
\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}
is uniformly continuous, the modulus of continuity
\bar{\omega}(\Delta):=\sup\{|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}(x)-\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}(x^{\prime})|:x,x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\|x-x^{\prime}\|\leq\Delta\}
satisfies
\lim_{\Delta\searrow 0}\bar{\omega}(\Delta)=0
. Hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\tr(\xi_{\textup{II}.k}\xi_{\textup{II}.k}^{\top})\,\big|\,(X_{i})_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}},\hat{\rho}_{k}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})K_{h}(X_{j}-x_{0})\bigl(Q_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\top}Q_{h}(X_{j}-x_{0})\bigr)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\cdot\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})(\varepsilon_{j}\,|\,X_{j})\,\big|\,X_{i},X_{j},\hat{\rho}_{k}\bigr\}

\displaystyle\leq e^{2d}\biggl(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})K_{h}(X_{j}-x_{0})|

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\cdot\big|\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})(\hat{\rho}_{k}^{\prime}-\rho^{\prime})(\varepsilon_{j}\,|\,X_{j})\,\big|\,X_{i},X_{j},\hat{\rho}_{k}\bigr\}\big|\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq e^{2d}\biggl(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\cdot\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}^{1/2}(X_{i})\biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2e^{2d}|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}(x_{0})|\biggl(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+2e^{2d}\biggl(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\cdot|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}(X_{i})-\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}(x_{0})|^{1/2}\biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2e^{2d}\bigl(|\mathcal{E}_{\hat{\rho}^{\prime},k,\mathrm{sq}}(x_{0})|+\bar{\omega}(h)\bigr)\biggl(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}|K_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})|\biggr)^{2}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1),

by Assumption
A1
. Therefore by e.g.
39
, it follows that
\xi_{\textup{II}.k}\xi_{\textup{II}.k}^{\top}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
.

Term
\boldsymbol{\xi_{\textup{III}}}
:

For
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+b_{n}\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\tr(\xi_{\textup{III}}\xi_{\textup{III}}^{\top})\bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{\tr\big({\pi_{h}}(X-x_{0}){\pi_{h}}(X-x_{0})^{\top}\big)\bigl\{\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\bigr\}^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{K_{h}^{2}(X-x_{0})\|Q_{h}(X-x_{0})\|_{2}^{4}\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(\bigl\{\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\bigr\}^{2}\,\big|\,X\big)\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}C_{2}e^{2d}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{K_{h}^{2}(X-x_{0})\bigr\}\leq C_{2}C_{X}e^{2d}R_{2}(K)\cdot\frac{1}{nh^{d}}.

Therefore
\xi_{\textup{III}}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh^{d}}}\big)=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
.

Term
\boldsymbol{\xi_{\textup{IV}}}
:
For
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+b_{n}\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\displaystyle\xi_{\textup{IV}}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)Q(\nu)Q(\nu)^{\top}\big\{\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)-\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})p_{X}(x_{0})\big\}\,d\nu.

Thus, by the continuity of
\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}
and
p_{X}
at
x_{0}
, and since

\biggl\|\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)Q(\nu)Q(\nu)^{\top}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0}+h\nu)p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu\biggr\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{2}^{2}C_{X}^{2}e^{2d}R_{1}^{2}(K),

it follows by the dominated convergence theorem that
\xi_{\textup{IV}}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
.

We conclude that
\hat{\Omega}(\boldsymbol{\tau})=\Omega+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
, as required.
∎

Lemma 15
.

We adopt the setup of Theorem
1
.

(i)

We have

\frac{1}{p_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{K_{h}(X-x_{0})e_{0}^{*}(X)\bigr\}=h^{\beta^{*}}\,B(f,x_{0},K,h)+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigl(h^{\beta^{*}}\bigr),

where

B(f,x_{0},K,h):=h^{\beta_{0}^{*}-\beta^{*}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)\nu^{\alpha}g_{\alpha,\beta_{0}^{*},x_{0},h}(\nu)\,d\nu

and

g_{\alpha,\beta_{0}^{*},x_{0},h}(\nu):=\beta_{0}^{*}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\beta_{0}^{*}-1}\bigl\{\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0}+th\nu)-\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0})\bigr\}\,dt.

(ii)

There exists
C_{B}(K,d,\beta_{0}^{*},L)>0
such that

\sup_{f\in\mathcal{H}(\beta,L)}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}_{n}}|B(f,x_{0},K,h)|\leq C_{B}(K,d,\beta_{0}^{*},L),

and hence

\sup_{f\in\mathcal{H}(\beta,L)}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}_{n}}\frac{1}{p_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{K_{h}(X-x_{0})e_{0}^{*}(X)\bigr\}=O\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big).

Proof.

(i)
Recalling the definition of
e_{0}^{*}
from (
52
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{0}}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{K_{h}(X

\displaystyle-x_{0})e_{0}^{*}(X)\bigr\}=\frac{h^{\beta_{0}^{*}}}{p_{0}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)Q(\nu)\nu^{\alpha}\,g_{\alpha,\beta_{0}^{*},x_{0},h}(\nu)\,p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)\,d\nu.

Now, for
n
large enough that
\mathcal{X}+b_{n}\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)\subseteq\mathcal{X}^{\circ}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\frac{h^{\beta_{0}^{*}}}{p_{0}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)\nu^{\alpha}g_{\alpha,\beta_{0}^{*},x_{0},h}(\nu)\bigl\{p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)-p_{X}(x_{0})\bigr\}\,d\nu\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{h^{\beta_{0}^{*}}}{p_{0}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{\beta_{0}^{*}}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}|K(\nu)|\cdot\big|\nu^{\alpha}\big|\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0}+th\nu)-\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0})\bigr|\,dt\cdot\bigl|p_{X}(x_{0}+h\nu)-p_{X}(x_{0})\bigr|\,d\nu

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{X}Lh^{\beta^{*}+(\beta_{X}\wedge 1)}}{p_{0}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{\beta_{0}^{*}}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}|K(\nu)|\|\nu\|^{\beta^{*}-\beta_{0}^{*}+(\beta_{X}\wedge 1)}\,d\nu

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{X}Ld^{\beta_{0}^{*}}R_{1}(K)}{c_{X}(\beta_{0}^{*}-1)!}\,h^{\beta^{*}+(\beta_{X}\wedge 1)}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\big(h^{\beta^{*}}\big),

which establishes
(i)
.

(ii)

By identical arguments to those employed in
(i)
,

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in\mathcal{H}(\beta,L)}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}_{n}}\Bigg|h^{\beta_{0}^{*}-\beta^{*}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}

\displaystyle K(\nu)\nu^{\alpha}g_{\alpha,\beta_{0}^{*},x_{0},h}(\nu)\,d\nu\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Ld^{\beta_{0}^{*}}R_{1}(K)}{(\beta_{0}^{*}-1)!}=:C_{B}(K,d,\beta_{0}^{*},L),

(64)

so
(ii)
follows.
∎

Lemma 16
.

Let
B_{q}:=\{\nu\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\|\nu\|_{q}\leq 1\}
. For
\nu\in B_{q}
,

\|Q(\nu)\|_{1}\vee\|Q(\nu)\|_{2}^{2}\leq e^{d}.

Proof.

We have
|\nu_{r}|\leq\|\nu\|_{q}^{q}\leq 1
for every
r\in[d]
, and recall that the multinomial theorem states that

\biggl(\sum_{r=1}^{d}x_{r}\biggr)^{\ell}=\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\|\alpha\|_{1}=\ell}\frac{\ell!}{\alpha!}x^{\alpha}

(65)

for
x=(x_{1},\ldots,x_{d})^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\ell\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
. Thus, by Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\|Q(\nu)\|_{1}=\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq p}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\prod_{r=1}^{d}|\nu_{r}|^{\alpha_{r}}\leq\sum_{\ell=0}^{p}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\|\alpha\|_{1}=\ell}\frac{1}{\alpha!}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{p}\frac{d^{\ell}}{\ell!}\leq e^{d}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\|Q(\nu)\|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq p}\frac{1}{(\alpha!)^{2}}\prod_{r=1}^{d}|\nu_{r}|^{2\alpha_{r}}\leq\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq p}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\leq e^{d},

as required.
∎

Lemma 17
.

For
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
\|x-x_{0}\|\leq h
, we have

\displaystyle|e_{0}^{*}(x)|\leq\frac{Ld^{\beta_{0}^{*}}}{\beta_{0}^{*}!}h^{\beta^{*}}=:C_{e^{*}}(L,d,\beta^{*})h^{\beta^{*}},

\displaystyle|e_{0}(x)|\leq L(e^{hd}-1)+\frac{Ld^{\beta_{0}^{*}}}{\beta_{0}^{*}!}h^{\beta^{*}}=:C_{e}(L,d,\beta^{*},h)h^{1\vee\beta^{*}}.

Proof.

By analogous arguments to Lemma
16
,

|e_{0}^{*}(x)|\leq L\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{\beta_{0}^{*}}{\alpha!}\|x-x_{0}\|^{\beta^{*}-\beta_{0}^{*}}|(x-x_{0})^{\alpha}|\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{\beta_{0}^{*}-1}\,dt\leq Lh^{\beta^{*}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}=\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}=\frac{Ld^{\beta_{0}^{*}}}{\beta_{0}^{*}!}h^{\beta^{*}}.

Further,

\displaystyle|e_{0}(x)|\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
1\leq\|\alpha\|_{1}\leq\beta_{0}^{*}\end{subarray}}\!\!\frac{1}{\alpha!}\,\bigl|\partial^{\alpha}f(x_{0})(x-x_{0})^{\alpha}\bigr|+|e_{0}^{*}(x)|

\displaystyle\leq L\!\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}:\\
\|\alpha\|_{1}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{h^{\|\alpha\|_{1}}}{\alpha!}+|e_{0}^{*}(x)|=L(e^{hd}-1)+|e_{0}^{*}(x)|,

as required.
∎

Lemma 18
.

Recall the triangular array
Z_{n,1},\ldots,Z_{n,n}
defined in the proof of Theorem
1
(see (
42
)), define
\sigma_{P}^{2}\equiv\sigma_{P,n}^{2}:=\mathrm{Var}_{P}(Z_{n,1})
. Then

\sup_{P\in\mathcal{P}}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}_{n}}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}_{P}\biggl(\frac{1}{n^{1/2}\sigma_{P}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{n,i}\leq t\biggr)-\Phi(t)\bigg|\rightarrow 0

(66)

as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Proof.

For
x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}
, define

a:=\frac{1}{p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}\,\mathrm{e}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}},\qquad b:=\frac{1}{p_{0}}\in\mathbb{R}

so that

\displaystyle Z_{n,i}

\displaystyle:=a^{\top}\varphi(X_{i})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})+b\,\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})\varepsilon_{i}

\displaystyle\phantom{:}=a^{\top}\iota_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})+\big\{-a^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon_{i}\,|\,X_{i})+b\varepsilon_{i}\big\}\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X_{i}-x_{0})

(67)

for
i\in[n]
. Note
\mathbb{E}_{P}(Z_{n,i})=0
, and since
\iota_{h}(\cdot)\kappa_{h,\lambda}(\cdot)=0
,

\displaystyle h^{d}\sigma_{P}^{2}

\displaystyle=h^{d}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\bigl(a^{\top}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})\bigr)^{2}\rho^{2}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\big\}+h^{d}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\big(a^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)-b\varepsilon\big)^{2}\kappa_{h,\lambda}^{2}(X-x_{0})\big\}

\displaystyle=\big(a^{\top}s_{2}(K)a\big)\,p_{0}\zeta_{\rho^{2}}(x_{0})+\mathbb{E}_{P}\big\{\big(a^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)-b\varepsilon\big)^{2}\,\big|\,X=x_{0}\big\}p_{0}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{R_{2}(K)}{p_{0}}\cdot\frac{\zeta_{\rho^{2}}(x_{0})}{\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}^{2}(x_{0})}+\frac{R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})}{p_{0}}\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)}{\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}-\varepsilon\bigg)^{2}\,\bigg|\,X=x_{0}\bigg\}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1),

where we have used the fact that
\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})\big(a^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\big)=b+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)
.
Thus

\sigma_{P}^{2}=\frac{1}{p_{0}h^{d}}\bigg\{R_{2}(K)V_{P}(\rho)+\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)}{\zeta_{\rho^{\prime}}(x_{0})}-\varepsilon\bigg)^{2}\,\bigg|\,X=x_{0}\bigg\}R_{2}(\kappa_{\lambda})\bigg\}+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{h^{d}}\bigg).

(68)

By Lemma
19
, it suffices to verify the uniform Lyapunov condition

\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(|Z_{n,1}|^{2+\delta}\big)}{n^{\delta/2}\sigma_{P}^{2+\delta}}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1).

(69)

Note first that

\inf_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\inf_{P\in\mathcal{P}}\frac{R_{2}(K)V_{P}(\rho)}{p_{0}}\geq\frac{c_{1}R_{2}(K)}{C_{2}C_{X}},

and so

\frac{1}{\sigma_{P}^{2}}=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(h^{d}).

(70)

For the
(2+\delta)
th absolute moment upper bound, first note the inequalities
|a^{\top}\iota_{h}(\cdot)|\leq K(\cdot)/(c_{X}c_{1})
and
|b|\leq 1/c_{X}
. Thus by Lemma
16
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(

\displaystyle|Z_{n,1}|^{2+\delta}\big)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{|a^{\top}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})|^{2+\delta}|\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)|^{2+\delta}+\big|a^{\top}\mu(x_{0})\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)-b\varepsilon\big|^{2+\delta}|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})|^{2+\delta}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{1+\delta}\mathbb{E}_{P}\Big\{|a^{\top}\iota_{h}(X-x_{0})|^{2+\delta}|\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)|^{2+\delta}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2^{1+\delta}\big(\|a\|_{2}^{2+\delta}\|\mu(x_{0})\|_{2}^{2+\delta}|\rho(\varepsilon\,|\,X)|^{2+\delta}+|b|^{2+\delta}|\varepsilon|^{2+\delta}\big)|\kappa_{h,\lambda}(X-x_{0})|^{2+\delta}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{1+\delta}\biggl[\biggl(\frac{1}{c_{X}c_{1}}\biggr)^{2+\delta}C_{X}C_{1}R_{2+\delta}(K)+2^{1+\delta}\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{e^{d/2}}{c_{X}c_{1}\Lambda_{\min}\bigl(s_{1}(K)\bigr)}\biggr)^{2+\delta}C_{X}C_{1}\|u(K)\|_{2}^{2+\delta}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{C_{3}|\mathrm{e}_{1}^{\top}s_{1}(K)^{-1}u(K)|^{2+\delta}}{c_{X}^{2+\delta}}\biggr\}R_{2+\delta}(\kappa_{\lambda})+o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1)\biggr]\cdot\frac{1}{h^{d(1+\delta)}}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}\bigg(\frac{1}{h^{d(1+\delta)}}\bigg).

(71)

Combining (
70
) and (
71
), the uniform Lyapunov condition (
69
) holds, and this completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 19
.

Let
(\psi_{P,n,i})_{P\in\mathcal{P},n\in\mathbb{N},i\in[n]}
be a triangular array of real-valued random functions
\psi_{P,n,i}:\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{H}_{n}\to\mathbb{R}
such that for any
P\in\mathcal{P}
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we have that
(\psi_{P,n,i})_{i\in[n]}
are independent,
\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\psi_{P,n,i}(x_{0},h)\bigr)=0
for all
i\in[n]
and
(P,x_{0},h)\in\mathcal{P}\times\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{H}_{n}
and there exists
\delta>0
such that the uniform Lyapunov condition

\sup_{P\in\mathcal{P}}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}_{n}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{P}\big(|\psi_{P}(x_{0},h)|^{2+\delta}\big)}{n^{\delta/2}\Var_{P}^{1+\delta/2}\psi_{P}(x_{0},h)}\rightarrow 0

holds. For each
n\in\mathbb{N}
, let
S_{P,n}:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{P,n,i}
. Then

\sup_{P\in\mathcal{P}}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}_{n}}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\biggl|\mathbb{P}_{P}\biggl(\frac{S_{P,n}(x_{0},h)}{\Var_{P}^{1/2}S_{P,n}(x_{0},h)}\leq t\biggr)-\Phi(t)\biggr|\rightarrow 0.

Proof.

This follows by identical arguments to
74

alongside a uniform version of Slutsky’s lemma
(e.g.
51
, Lemma 20)
.
∎

Lemma 20
.

Adopting the setup of Theorem
1
, the measurable sequence
\hat{\theta}=(\hat{\theta}_{n})
from Algorithm
1
satisfies

\hat{\theta}-\theta_{0}=o_{\mathcal{P},\mathcal{X},\mathcal{H},\Lambda}(1).

Proof.

Define
\Psi:\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}
and
\hat{\Psi}_{n}:\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}
by

\Psi(\theta):=p_{X}(x_{0})\int_{\mathcal{B}_{0}(1)}K(\nu)Q(\nu)\,\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl\{\rho\bigl(\varepsilon+Q(\nu)^{\top}(\theta_{0}-\theta)\,|\,X\bigr)\,\big|\,X=x_{0}\bigr\}\,d\nu,

and

\hat{\Psi}_{n}({\theta}):=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\mathcal{K}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}_{k}}\hat{\varphi}_{k}(X_{i})\hat{\rho}_{k}\Bigl(\varepsilon_{i}+\bigl(e_{0}(X_{i})+Q_{h}(X_{i}-x_{0})^{\top}(\theta_{0}-\theta)\bigr)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(X_{i})\\
-\bigl(\hat{f}_{k}^{\mathrm{LP}}(X_{i})-f(X_{i})+\hat{c}_{k}(x_{0})\bigr)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(\lambda h)\setminus\mathcal{B}_{x_{0}}(h)}(X_{i})\,\Big|\,X_{i}\Bigr).

Taking
\eta>0
from Assumption
A1
, let
\hat{\theta}^{*}
denote the unique root of
\hat{\Psi}_{n}
in
\{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}:\|\theta-\theta_{0}\|_{\infty}\leq\eta\}
.
Since
s_{1}(K)
is invertible, there exists an orthonormal basis
(t_{m})_{m\in[\bar{p}]}
for
\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}
with
t_{m}^{\top}s_{1}(K)t_{m}\neq 0
for all
m\in[\bar{p}]
.
For
m\in[\bar{p}]
, define
\psi_{m}:[0,\infty)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
by

\psi_{m}(\lambda):=t_{m}^{\top}\Psi(\theta_{0}+\lambda t_{m}).

Then
\psi_{m}(0)=0
and, using Assumption
(A1.5)
, we may differentiate under the integral sign to obtain

|\psi_{m}^{\prime}(0)|=p_{X}(x_{0})\bigl|t_{m}^{\top}s_{1}(K)t_{m}\mathbb{E}_{P}\bigl(\rho^{\prime}(\varepsilon\,|\,X)\,|\,X=x_{0}\bigr)\bigr|\geq c_{X}c_{1}|t_{m}^{\top}s_{1}(K)t_{m}|>0.

Therefore, there exists
\lambda^{*}>0
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda^{*}]
and
m\in[\bar{p}]
,

\bigl(t_{m}^{\top}\Psi(\theta_{0}+\lambda t_{m})\bigr)\bigl(t_{m}^{\top}\Psi(\theta_{0}-\lambda t_{m})\bigr)<0.

Take arbitrary
\epsilon,\eta^{\prime}\in(0,\eta)
. By the Poincaré–Miranda theorem
(
13
, Theorem 17.1.1)
, if
\|\hat{\theta}^{*}-\theta_{0}\|_{\infty}>\epsilon
then there exists
m\in[\bar{p}]
such that

\bigl(t_{m}^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}+\epsilon t_{m})\bigr)\bigl(t_{m}^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}-\epsilon t_{m})\bigr)>0.

Taking a union bound over these events, for any
\epsilon,\eta_{1}>0
, and defining
\epsilon^{\prime}:=\epsilon\wedge\lambda^{*}\wedge\eta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{P}

\displaystyle(\|\hat{\theta}^{*}-\theta_{0}\|_{\infty}>\epsilon)\leq\mathbb{P}_{P}(\|\hat{\theta}^{*}-\theta_{0}\|_{\infty}>\epsilon^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m=1}^{\bar{p}}\Bigl\{\mathbb{P}_{P}\bigl(t_{m}^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})>0,\;t_{m}^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})>0\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{P}\bigl(t_{m}^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})<0,\;t_{m}^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})<0\bigr)\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m=1}^{\bar{p}}\Bigl\{\mathbb{P}_{P}\bigl(\bigl|t_{m}^{\top}\bigl\{\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})-\Psi(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})\bigr\}\bigr|>\eta_{1}\bigr)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{t_{m}^{\top}\Psi(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})>-\eta_{1}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{t_{m}^{\top}\Psi(\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})>-\eta_{1}\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}_{P}\bigl(\bigl|t_{m}^{\top}\bigl\{\hat{\Psi}_{n}(\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})-\Psi(\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})\bigr\}\bigr|>\eta_{1}\bigr)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{t_{m}^{\top}\Psi(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})<\eta_{1}\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{t_{m}^{\top}\Psi(\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{\prime}\,t_{m})<\eta_{1}\}}\Bigr\}.

Similarly to the proof of Theorem
1
, for
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{\bar{p}}
with
